# Hypothesis register

Every claim that is not yet verified is a hypothesis with a test or an acceptance-kit request that
settles it.

- Ids keep three prefixes: `H-A-` (second backend), `H-G-` (general and model) and `H-K-` (KiCad).
- Rows about a tool that acts on KiCad files use `H-K-`, and routing rows use the `H-K-KRT-` prefix. Specctra
  DSN/SES rows are `H-G-DSN-*`, with backend `specctra`.
- A refuted row keeps its id. Its level is the level of the run that refuted the claim, and its result
  starts with `refuted; superseded by <successor id>`. A refuted row never counts as release support.
  A result that starts with other text, such as `partly refuted`, is not a refutation.
- An author-report row (level `ALTIUM-VERIFIED(author-report; …)`) never raises an operation to
  verified.
- `tests/unit/test_hypotheses_register.py` checks this register and every hypothesis id cited in the
  repository's live text; `fenolite.verify` reads it.

Reserved families: ids that no row extends yet but that later changes will register.
A family leaves this table once its rows are registered and name their settling tests: `H-A-WRITE-*` and
`H-A-PH-*` left it with change c0091, which names the kit steps of their eight rows.

| family | backend | rows for | owner |

| H-K-COPPER-SHAPES | kicad | KiCad's DRC judges copper as Fenolite's thick shapes do (a track is its centre segment swept by its width, an arc likewise within 2 µm, a via a disc of its diameter on each spanned layer, a pad the core and width of the board-frame record), so the `clearance` or clean verdict of each pair agrees at `c − 10 µm`, at `c` and at `c + 10 µm` (S-0010, S-0038) | KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x) | `tests/kicad/copper/test_copper_parity.py -k parity` | on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, every `copper-parity-<kind>` probe = `equal`; boundary rows recorded | confirmed with local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6 (macOS) and 9.0.9 (pinned image, local run) on 2026-10-04: the seven `copper-parity-<kind>` probes are `equal` (track–track, arc–track, via–track, via–via, SMD pad–track, pad–pad, through-hole pad–track on `B.Cu`), each under a rule, a class and a board-minimum clearance; no row overlaps or touches, because KiCad's verdict on touching copper is not repeatable (`H-K-VIA-RENET`); boundary rows: `copper-boundary-1um` `present`, `copper-boundary-1nm` `absent` on both versions, so KiCad applies a tolerance where Fenolite is strict; the rows cover the benches only, so `checks.copper.EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED`; confirmed again by the `kicad-9` and `kicad-10` jobs (run https://github.com/lgili/Fenolite/actions/runs/37178050484) | 2026-10-04 |
| H-K-COPPER-RESOLVE | kicad | Beyond what c0026 measures (a board-wide rule governs over the classes and below the board minimum), KiCad's clearance in force for a pair follows Fenolite's resolver: without a custom rule the larger clearance of the two nets' classes applies; a rule with a condition stands to the classes and to the board minimum as an unconditional one does (`H-K-PRO-MIN-CLASS`, `H-K-PRO-MIN-RULE-2`); an `ignore` rule silences the pair; and a rule on `A.Type == 'Track'` governs arc tracks (S-0010, S-0038) | KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x) | `tests/kicad/copper/test_copper_parity.py -k resolve` | on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, every `copper-resolve-<case>` probe = `equal`, with the switches taken from c0026's tables for the running major | confirmed with local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6 (macOS) and 9.0.9 (pinned image, local run) on 2026-10-04: the six `copper-resolve-<case>` probes are `equal` (`rule-below-class`, `rule-above-class`, `two-classes`, `ignore`, `arc-kind`, `floor-above-rule`), each with a row that KiCad reports and a row that it does not, with `rules_over_classes` true and `floor_over_rules` false as c0026's tables give for both majors; confirmed again by the `kicad-9` and `kicad-10` jobs (run https://github.com/lgili/Fenolite/actions/runs/37178050484) | 2026-10-04 |

| H-A-SCHBIN-MINI | altium | When Altium Designer builds the sheet hierarchy of a project, it takes a module sheet as a child only if the sheet's `FileHeader` stream is stored in regular sectors (4096 bytes or more); a sheet whose `FileHeader` lies in the compound file's mini stream stays outside the hierarchy until Altium loads it in full. With one hidden sheet parameter that brings `FileHeader` to 4096 bytes, the sheet is a child at once (S-0145, S-0130, S-0187, S-0188) | ALTIUM-VERIFIED(author-report; AD 26.5; 2026-10-03; no artefact) | kit request (author report, step H7 of Part H of `docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`, with its variants) | the rebuilt board example shows both module sheets under the top sheet after a compile, without any dialog; in the variants built without the parameter, the sheet outside the hierarchy is each time the one under 4096 bytes | refuted; superseded by H-A-SCH-HIER-ORDER; on 2026-10-03 (AD 26.5, a trial licence on the maintainer's own PC), on fresh copies and after "Validate PCB Project" alone: the board example with its two small sheets padded to 4096 bytes still had its second module sheet outside the hierarchy, and so had the first build with the same padding (variant c); the variant with the small sheet moved to the other module (variant d) failed as well; and with the module of `D1` listed first (variant e) that small sheet was a child and the large sheet `driver` was outside. The size of `FileHeader` is therefore not the cause, and Altium reads a sheet stored in the mini stream. The padding and its hidden parameter are removed | 2026-10-03 |

| H-K-PARITY-TYPES | kicad | `pcb drc --schematic-parity` gives, for a footprint whose reference was renamed, one `missing_footprint` and one `extra_footprint`; for a changed value or a changed library id, one `footprint_symbol_mismatch` each; for a pad on another net, one `net_conflict`; for a footprint that takes another's reference, one `duplicate_footprints` and one `missing_footprint`; for a copied footprint with a new reference, one `extra_footprint`; and nothing for a project that agrees (S-0020, S-0029) | KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x) | `tests/kicad/check/test_parity_probes.py` | probes `parity-type-<edit>` `equal` on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, for the edits `none`, `ref`, `value`, `libid`, `net`, `dup`, `extra` and `value-and-libid` | confirmed on 2026-10-06 with the local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6 (macOS) and 9.0.9 (pinned image, local run), as recorded in the probe files: every probe is `equal` on the built blink of each major, and the same edits give the same types on the `pic_programmer` demo of each tag (at tag 9.0.9.1 on top of the 61 mismatches of the published demo). | H-A-VER-RTA3 | altium | A PCB document that Altium saved, imported, written by Fenolite and imported again gives an equal model inside `RT_A3_SCOPE`, once the items that the write reports as not written are taken out of the first model | CORPUS-VERIFIED | `tests/corpus/test_altium_rta3.py` | equal on every listed corpus document, from at least three repositories; the heavy document, which runs only with `FENOLITE_HEAVY=1`, is equal since an arc that was read keeps the centre, the radius and the angles of its record (529 arcs written, each point back at 0 nm); the level was raised to `CORPUS-VERIFIED` by the maintainer's decision of 2026-10-07, the row's criterion being met. **What the level means:** Fenolite reads its own rewrite of a public Altium PCB document back to an equal model inside the written scope, and KiCad's importer reads the rewrite as Fenolite does at the levels 1 to 5 of `equivalent` (`H-A-VER-RTA3-KICAD`, measured on the seven documents that are not heavy). **What it does not mean:** it says nothing about Altium opening a written file, which stays `INFERRED` until the kit run (c0091, c0092); no `roundtrip_exact` cell is set and no write kind leaves `experimental` by it. **What the scope leaves out:** the graphics of footprints (until c0126), component bodies (until c0121), and the model items that are not written, counted per kind in the table "PCB documents" of that section (shapes on copper, poured fills, pads with a per-layer stack, items on layers without a layer in the written document); a rewrite is not a copy of the document; a stage and a verdict stay `INFERRED`, because the import's evidence is; earlier supporting data 2026-10-06 by `tests/corpus/test_altium_rta3.py` (run recorded in `docs/evidence/altium-roundtrip.md`, section "RT-A3"): the seven PCB documents that are not heavy, from six repositories, are equal inside the scope (433 footprints, 1573 pads, 3144 tracks, 23 arcs, 1115 vias and 42 zones written and equal); the heavy document differs in 7 of its 517 written arcs, whose points come back 3 nm or more away (an arc record holds a centre, a radius and angles, each rounded to a unit; the model holds three points), and in nothing else (2115 pads, 8355 tracks, 1770 vias, 27 zones equal); the row was pending because of that document until change c0127; what a rewrite does not hold is counted per kind on the page (footprint graphics, bodies, shapes on copper, poured fills, pads with a per-layer stack; | H-K-DRC-LIMITS | kicad | `kicad-cli pcb drc --format json --severity-all` writes at most 499 entries of `clearance` (9.0.9: 499 to 508) and of `unconnected_items`, and at most 199 entries of every other type measured, per run and per type, on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 (thirteen types on 10.0.6; twelve on 9.0.9, which writes no `hole_clearance` entry for the bench's construct, so that limit is not measured there); a type under its limit is written in full; `--all-track-errors` does not lift the limit of `track_dangling`; no key of the report says that a type was cut: ten top-level keys on 10.0.6, nine on 9.0.9, without `ignored_checks` (S-0020, S-0029) | KICAD-VERIFIED (10.0.x) | `tests/kicad/check/test_drc_limits.py` | probes `drc-limit-<type>` for the 13 types of the authored bench at 700 copies, `drc-limit-below` at 150 copies, `drc-limit-all-track-errors` and `drc-limit-keys`, `equal` on both majors: each count equal to `REPORT_LIMITS[major]`, `clearance` on 9.0.9 at least 499 and below 700; corrected on 2026-10-08 from what 9.0.9 measured: `drc-limit-hole_clearance` is `different` on 9.0.9 (no entry), the type is outside `MEASURED_TYPES[9]`, and `drc-limit-keys` compares with the keys of the running major; a type whose count differs between two runs leaves `per_type` and is named here | pending for 9.0.9; the 10.0.6 half is confirmed. First record, 2026-10-05 (review branch, before the bench of this change): on 10.0.6, thirteen benches of 700 copies gave 499 `clearance`, 499 `unconnected_items` and 199 of each of the eleven other types, 150 copies gave 150, and `--all-track-errors` left `track_dangling` at 199; on 9.0.9 (pinned image), one board of nine benches gave 500 `clearance`, 499 `unconnected_items` and 199 of each of `copper_edge_clearance`, `courtyards_overlap`, `hole_to_hole`, `lib_footprint_issues`, `silk_overlap`, `track_dangling`, `track_width` and `via_dangling`. Confirmed on 2026-10-07 with local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6 (macOS) by `tests/kicad/check/test_drc_limits.py` on the authored bench `tests/kicad/check/_limitsbench.py` (20 passed; the 16 `drc-limit-*` probes are recorded `equal` in `docs/evidence/kicad/probes/10.0.6.json`): one board of 13 × 700 constructs gives 499, 499 and 199 for each of the eleven others and no entry of a type outside the bench; 150 copies give 150 for `track_dangling`, `silk_overlap` and `unconnected_items`; `--all-track-errors` gives 199; the report has the ten known top-level keys. Not yet recorded on 9.0.9 with this bench (the pinned image was not run; the `kicad-9` job records the probes), and `hole_clearance`, `annular_width` and `shorting_items` have no 9.0.9 measurement at all, so the level stays `INFERRED` until then (`docs/evidence/kicad-check.md`, "DRC report limits per type"). Run on 2026-10-08 on `kicad-cli` 9.0.9 (the pinned image `kicad/kicad:9.0.9@sha256:e638b79b…`, linux/amd64, local run; the `kicad-9` job of CI has not run it yet), the level stays `INFERRED`: 14 of the 16 probes are `equal` in `docs/evidence/kicad/probes/9.0.9.json` (499 `clearance`, 499 `unconnected_items`, 199 of each of the ten other types counted, `annular_width` and `shorting_items` among them; 150 at 150 copies; `--all-track-errors` leaves 199), and two are `different`: `drc-limit-hole_clearance`, because 9.0.9 reports no `hole_clearance` entry at all for the bench's construct (0, not 199), and `drc-limit-keys`, because the 9.0.9 report has nine top-level keys, without `ignored_checks`. Three tests of `test_drc_limits.py` fail there for these two reasons; the limit of `hole_clearance` on 9.0.9 is still not measured. Corrected per major later on 2026-10-08 (c0141, design, "Found on 2026-10-08"): the statement, the criterion, the bench and the tests now say what each major measured, and no measured outcome was changed to pass. `drc-limit-hole_clearance` stays `different` on 9.0.9 and its test expects that: a diagnosis in the image showed no `hole_clearance` entry for a pad beside an unplated hole in one footprint or in two, with or without a net, at 3 and at 700 copies, where 10.0.6 gives 3 and 199; 9.0.9 does report the type between a via hole and a track (`H-K-DRU-KIND-2`), a construct the bench does not hold because it would add entries of other types. `backends.kicad.drc.MEASURED_TYPES` names the measured types per major and a unit test holds it to the probe files; on 9.0.9 `hole_clearance` falls under the assumed `others` = 199, as every type outside the bench does on both majors. `drc-limit-keys` now compares with the key list of the running major and was recorded again on 9.0.9 in the image: `equal` (nine keys), in place of `different`; no other row moved. In the image (the pinned image `kicad/kicad:9.0.9@sha256:e638b79b…`, linux/amd64, local run; the `kicad-9` job of CI has not run it yet) `tests/kicad/check/test_drc_limits.py` gives 20 passed, as on the local 10.0.6; 15 of the 16 probes are `equal` on 9.0.9 and 16 on 10.0.6. Open: a construct of one type alone that 9.0.9 reports as `hole_clearance`; level by the strict reading (2026-10-08): the criterion asks for `equal` on both majors and `drc-limit-hole_clearance` is `different` on 9.0.9, so the row is verified for 10.0.x; on 9.0.9 twelve of the thirteen types are measured and equal (local run in the pinned image, CI still to confirm), and the limit of `hole_clearance` is unmeasured there until a construct exists that 9.0.9 reports as that type alone | 2026-10-08 |

Measured with the local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6 (macOS) on 2026-10-07, as recorded in `docs/evidence/kicad/probes/10.0.6.json`: `copper-anchor-frame` = `equal` (36 marker vias on `Frame_Anchor` at 0° and 90° on the top and 30° on the bottom, anchored by `part.at` at the library positions of pads `1` and `3`, by `pad.at` inside pad `4` and on a 3 × 3 grid of 1 mm in pad `4`: each is named with the net of the pad its anchor names, and no `shorting_items` names one), `copper-anchor-frame-control` = `different` (two board points of the bottom part computed without the mirror are named with each other's nets), `copper-anchor-moved` = `absent` (after `fenolite place --move U1=40mm,24mm,90` and a rebuild the 9 vias and 8 track segments of the array are regenerated, and none is dangling or unconnected) and `copper-anchor-moved-control` = `present` (the same array given as board points stays behind: 9 `via_dangling`). Change c0001 (repository bootstrap) added no hypothesis. The first rows (`H-K-UNIT`, `H-A-UNIT`,
`H-G-ANGLE`) came with the design model (change c0004) and stay `INFERRED` until corpus files confirm them.

Change c0002 (CLI contract) adds no hypothesis: `capabilities` and `_echo` read no design format; their
evidence label is `UNVERIFIED` because no external oracle applies to them.

Change c0003 (IP hygiene) adds no hypothesis: it is policy and tooling, verified by its own tests.

Change c0005 (geometry kernel) adds `H-G-ROT-DIR`, `H-G-BOTTOM-PLACE`, `H-G-BOTTOM-STORE`, `H-G-FLIP`,
`H-G-PAD-ANGLE-ABS`, `H-G-ARC-ROUND`, `H-G-ARC-DIR` and `H-G-PTS-ARC`. They state KiCad-frame facts but
use the `H-G-*` prefix because the change's id allocation gives it that prefix; their backend column is
`kicad`.

Change c0006 (KiCad S-expression layer and slots) adds `H-K-SEXPR-LEX-10`, `H-K-SEXPR-LEX-9`,
`H-K-SEXPR-STRICT`, `H-K-SEXPR-ESCAPES`, `H-K-SEXPR-NUM-READ`, `H-K-SEXPR-NUM-WRITE`,
`H-K-SEXPR-NUM-CORPUS`, `H-K-FMT-INDENT`, `H-K-FMT-XYWRAP`, `H-K-FMT-ATOMWRAP`, `H-K-FMT-MIXED` and
`H-K-FMT-RESAVE`. The `H-K-FMT-*` layout rows other than `H-K-FMT-RESAVE` are measured by the typed
board writer change, which compares Fenolite's printer with KiCad's byte for byte.

Change c0007 (KiCad format versions and token inventory) adds `H-K-00`, `H-K-01`, `H-K-02`, `H-K-03` and
the `H-K-TOK-*` rows. `H-K-02` was settled when both KiCad jobs first ran green on GitHub (2026-10-01).

Change c0008 (KiCad libraries) adds the `H-K-LIB-*` rows. `H-K-LIB-EXTENDS` is settled by the schematic
backend change, and `H-K-LIB-RELPATH`, `-FALLBACK`, `-NESTED` and `-CONFIGHOME` by the follow-up change
that adds the DRC harness; their tests are placeholders until then.

Change c0009 (KiCad board backend, part 1) adds `H-K-PCB-READ`, `H-K-PCB-POS`, `H-K-PCB-UUID`,
`H-K-PCB-ZONE` and `H-K-UUID-KEEP`. Its corpus census feeds `H-K-UNIT`, `H-G-ANGLE` and
`H-K-SEXPR-NUM-CORPUS`; its export cross-checks give supporting data to `H-G-BOTTOM-PLACE`,
`H-G-PAD-ANGLE-ABS` and `H-G-FLIP`; and the header census of the 9.0.9.1 demos feeds
`H-K-TOK-CONSTANTS`. `H-K-UUID-KEEP` was refuted on 10.0.6, because a re-save removes teardrop zones
and `Footprint` properties and replaces `fp_text` items; its successor `H-K-UUID-KEEP-2` states the
observed rule, and its Fenolite-written half is settled by the board writer change (c0017).

Change c0014 (verification evidence) adds `H-K-LIB-DRC` (settled by the board writer change, c0017)
and `H-G-SHAPELY-GC` (settled by the boolean-backends change, v0.3) as pending rows, because only
archived changes cited them. It relabels the refuted rows `H-K-SEXPR-NUM-WRITE` and `H-K-TOK-FUTURE`
with the level of the run that refuted them and names their successors, and it registers the
author-report families `H-A-WRITE-*` and `H-A-PH-*` for the author's earlier second-backend writer.

Change c0017 (KiCad board backend, part 2) adds `H-K-PCB-WRITE`, `H-K-GENVER` and `H-K-DRC-JSON`. Its
flip oracle settles `H-G-BOTTOM-STORE`, `H-G-FLIP`, `H-G-PAD-ANGLE-ABS` and `H-K-LIB-DRC` from KiCad's
library-parity check with negative controls, with `pcb export pos` and IPC-D-356 as consistency checks;
the corpus comparison planned by c0005 becomes optional supporting data for c0021. It settles the
Fenolite-written half of `H-K-UUID-KEEP-2` (the test that the refuted `H-K-UUID-KEEP` names).

Change c0018 (KiCad footprint writer and custom rules) adds `H-K-DRU-ORDER`, `H-K-DRU-DIALECT`,
`H-K-DRU-COND`, `H-K-DRU-GLOB`, `H-K-DRU-QUOTE` and `H-K-DRU-KIND`, settled by the rules oracle in
`tests/kicad/rules/`, where every proof is judged by the canary. It settles the footprint half of
`H-K-LIB-READ` by the footprint round trip over two corpus origins and the 9.0 half of
`H-K-SEXPR-ESCAPES` with the written escapes footprint; `H-K-TOK-CONSTANTS` stays `INFERRED` for
rules, because no KiCad command writes a rules file.

Change c0010 (KiCad project file) adds `H-K-PRO-VERSION`, `H-K-PRO-MIN`, `H-K-PRO-NETCLASS`,
`H-K-PRO-PATTERNS`, `H-K-PRO-FLOOR`, `H-K-PRO-TUNING` and `H-K-PRO-PRL`. `H-K-PRO-TUNING` answers c0007's
open question on tuning profiles: they live in the project file, not in the board. Its net-class
oracle also measured that `kicad-cli` 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 read a `.kicad_dru` without a project file,
which partly refutes the first half of `H-K-TOK-RULES-SILENT`.

Change c0011 (thin DSL and build) adds `H-K-BUILD-TRIAD`, `H-K-BUILD-CLASS`, `H-K-BUILD-LIBTABLE` and
`H-K-BUILD-PATHPROP`, probed first on boards made through the model API and then settled by the build
oracle in `tests/kicad/build/` on the CC0 blink. The built blink is a second design behind
`H-K-PRO-NETCLASS` and `H-K-LIB-DRC`. `lens.build.BUILD_EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED`: the rows cover the
blink, not every design.

Change c0054 (design-rule minimums in the design script) adds `H-K-DSL-MINIMUM`. It adds no writer: the
minimums reach KiCad through the rules lowering of c0018 and the board-setup minimums of c0026, whose
rows are verified on both majors, and the new row proves the whole path from a script to the DRC report.
Change c0012 (sheet templates) adds `H-K-WKS-SVG`, `H-K-WKS-FALLBACK`, `H-K-WKS-CORNER`,
`H-K-WKS-REPEAT`, `H-K-WKS-PAGE1`, `H-K-WKS-VARS`, `H-K-WKS-VALUES`, `H-K-WKS-BITMAP`, `H-K-WKS-RES`,
`H-K-PCB-PAPER`, `H-K-PRO-WKS` and `H-K-WKS-PCT`, settled by the drawing-sheet probes in
`tests/kicad/sheets/`, which judge a sheet by the texts and lines `kicad-cli` draws against a
same-session default-sheet control, never by the exit code. `H-K-WKS-FALLBACK` extends
`H-K-TOK-WKS-ORACLE` with the silent fallback for missing files. The probe spike refuted `H-K-WKS-RES` (KiCad
draws sub-micrometre worksheet lengths unchanged) and `H-K-PCB-PAPER` (the `User` page is truncated to
whole mils); `H-K-WKS-RES-2` and `H-K-PCB-PAPER-2` record what both majors draw.

Change c0026 (KiCad board-setup minimums) adds `H-K-PRO-MIN-KEYS`, `H-K-PRO-MIN-RULE`, `H-K-PRO-MIN-WRITE`
and `H-K-PRO-MIN-CLASS`; the stem is unrelated to c0010's row `H-K-PRO-MIN` (a minimal project file).
Its oracle refuted `H-K-PRO-MIN-RULE`: on `kicad-cli` 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 a board-wide custom rule governs
below the board-setup minimums, although the 9.0 and 10.0 manuals call them absolute.
`H-K-PRO-MIN-RULE-2` records the measured behaviour, so `lowering.FLOOR_OVER_RULES` is empty. A class
value stays under the minimum's floor (`H-K-PRO-FLOOR`), while a board-wide clearance rule overrides a
larger class clearance (`H-K-PRO-MIN-CLASS`).

Change c0032 (experimental Altium schematic writer) adds `H-A-SCH-OPEN`, `H-A-SCH-LINEEND`,
`H-A-SCH-UID`, `H-A-SCH-NETS`, `H-A-SCH-LINK`, `H-A-SCH-ECO`, `H-A-SCH-RELINK`, `H-A-SCH-UPDATE`,
`H-A-PRJ-OPEN` and `H-A-PRJ-KEEP`. They are settled only by the maintainer's author reports, following
`docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`: Part A on the committed sample files, Part B on a design and
libraries the maintainer may use. `kicad-cli` cannot read a `.SchDoc` (S-0132, S-0020), so no row has
an oracle. An author report never promotes an operation: `lens.altium.ALTIUM_BUILD_EVIDENCE` stays
`INFERRED`. `tests/unit/test_altium_rows.py` checks the form of these rows, because
`test_hypotheses_register.py` checks the author-report form only for `H-A-WRITE-*` and `H-A-PH-*`.
The maintainer's import of the ASCII sample in KiCad 10.0.6's schematic editor (2026-10-02) showed every
object and exactly the sample's nets; KiCad is not Altium, so it is supporting data and settles no row.
No Altium Designer report exists yet, so all ten rows stay pending.

Change c0027 (build properties and vendoring) adds `H-K-VENDOR-GLOBAL`, `H-K-VENDOR-SHADOW`,
`H-K-VENDOR-PROPS` and `H-K-VENDOR-DUPNAME`, probed first on boards made without its build code (stop
rules on `vendor-global` and `vendor-props`) and then settled by `tests/kicad/build/test_vendor_oracle.py`
on its own builds. The pre-proposal runs of its design (3 / 0 library violations without vendoring,
0 / 0 with it, on both majors) are supporting data. The `D_alt` and `D` controls of the shadow probes
show that `kicad-cli` 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 read a global table from `KICAD_CONFIG_HOME`, supporting data
for c0021's `H-K-LIB-CONFIGHOME`. `lens.build.PROPERTY_EVIDENCE` and `VENDOR_EVIDENCE` stay `INFERRED`.

Change c0033 (binary Altium schematic) adds `H-A-SCHBIN-CFB`, `H-A-SCHBIN-FRAME`, `H-A-SCHBIN-STORAGE`,
`H-A-SCHBIN-VIEWER` and `H-A-SCHBIN-AD`. The first four are settled by the maintainer's report of the
free Altium 365 Viewer on the committed binary sample (Part V of `docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`),
recorded as `ALTIUM-VERIFIED(author-report; A365 Viewer; <date>; no artefact)`, or by step A7 in Altium
Designer; `H-A-SCHBIN-AD` only by step A7. A Viewer render reads the file but neither compiles it nor
runs a change order. The maintainer's Viewer report of 2026-10-02 confirms `H-A-SCHBIN-CFB`, `H-A-SCHBIN-FRAME` and
`H-A-SCHBIN-STORAGE` by step V1; `H-A-SCHBIN-VIEWER` waits for V2, and `H-A-SCHBIN-AD` for step A7. The
Viewer's refusal of the ASCII sample is an observation about the Viewer only: the `H-A-SCH-*` rows
concern Altium Designer and stay pending. The container rules themselves are checked mechanically by the independent test
reader `tests/_cfb_read.py`; no row has an oracle.

Change c0034 (Altium schematic library) adds `H-A-SCHLIB-OPEN`, `H-A-SCHLIB-PIN`, `H-A-SCHLIB-PARTS`,
`H-A-SCHLIB-IMPLIDX`, `H-A-SCHLIB-PRJ`, `H-A-SCHLIB-SCHDOC`, `H-A-SCHLIB-MULTIPART`, `H-A-SCHLIB-UPDATE`,
`H-A-SCHLIB-SECTIONKEY`, `H-A-SCHLIB-KICAD` and `H-A-SCHLIB-KICAD9`. The first nine are settled only by the
maintainer's Part L report in Altium Designer (`docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`), with a licence the
maintainer may use for it (LEGAL.md block A, P4); the free Altium 365 Viewer refuses library files.
`H-A-SCHLIB-KICAD` is settled by the `kicad-cli sym upgrade` round trip of
`tests/kicad/altium/test_schlib_oracle.py` on 10.0.6, and `H-A-SCHLIB-KICAD9` by the same test in the
`kicad-9` job. The oracle checks what KiCad's importer reads, never an Altium-only key, so
`lens.altium.ALTIUM_BUILD_EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED`. The round trip passed with local `kicad-cli`
10.0.6 and with 9.0.9 from the pinned image on 2026-10-02, which confirms both oracle rows; the
`kicad-9` and `kicad-10` jobs of run https://github.com/lgili/Fenolite/actions/runs/37117800828 passed it again. The Part L
report of 2026-10-03 (AD 26.5) covers steps L4 and L5 of the sample and confirms `H-A-SCHLIB-PRJ`; the
other eight author-report rows stay pending. `H-A-WRITE-SCHLIB` is the roadmap row about the
author's earlier writer and is unchanged.

Change c0035 (Altium PCB library and document) adds `H-A-PCB-KICAD-LIB`, `H-A-PCB-KICAD-DOC`,
`H-A-PCB-LIB-OPEN`, `H-A-PCB-LIB-NAME`, `H-A-PCB-PAD`, `H-A-PCB-GRAPHICS`, `H-A-PCB-ECO`, `H-A-PCB-PRJ`,
`H-A-PCB-DOC-VIEWER`, `H-A-PCB-DOC-OPEN`, `H-A-PCB-DOC-LINK`, `H-A-PCB-DOC-NETS` and `H-A-PCB-DOC-BOTTOM`.
`H-A-PCB-KICAD-LIB` is settled by the `kicad-cli fp upgrade` round trip of
`tests/kicad/altium/test_pcblib_oracle.py` (10.0.6; the 9.0.9 result is recorded on the same row), and
`H-A-PCB-KICAD-DOC` by the `kicad-cli pcb import` of `tests/kicad/altium/test_pcbdoc_oracle.py` (10.0.x
only). `H-A-PCB-DOC-BOTTOM` is checked by the same import, then waits for the maintainer like the other
ten rows, which only an author report settles (`docs/evidence/altium-pcb.md`): Part P with the free Altium
365 Viewer, which lists `.PcbDoc` and not `.PcbLib`, and Part D in Altium Designer, recorded only with a
licence the maintainer may use for Fenolite (LEGAL.md block A, P4). The oracles check what KiCad's
importer reads, never an Altium-only key, so the build envelope stays `INFERRED`. `H-A-WRITE-PCBLIB` is
the roadmap row about the author's earlier writer and is unchanged.

Change c0036 (no-connect pins) adds `H-A-SCH-NC-RECORD`, `H-A-SCH-NC-ERC` and `H-A-SCH-NC-VIEWER`. All
three are settled only by the maintainer's report of Part N of `docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`: the
first two in Altium Designer, with a licence the maintainer may use for it (LEGAL.md block A, P4), the
third with the free Altium 365 Viewer. The report of 2026-10-03 (AD 26.5, a blink variant with two marked
pins, binary form) confirms `H-A-SCH-NC-RECORD` for the binary form; it shows the suppression at the
marked pins, but not that other pins keep their messages, so `H-A-SCH-NC-ERC` stays pending with that
observation, and `H-A-SCH-NC-VIEWER` is not reported. No source says that a directive on a pin's end without a wire
suppresses that pin's messages, so the sample holds an unmarked input pin as the positive control. An
author report never promotes an operation: `lens.altium.ALTIUM_BUILD_EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED`.
`H-K-CHECK-ERC` (c0013) keeps bounding `erc.lite`, which only gains an exemption.

Change c0037 (hierarchy and harnesses) adds `H-A-SCH-HIER-OPEN`, `H-A-SCH-HIER-PRJ`,
`H-A-SCH-HIER-COMPILE`, `H-A-SCH-HIER-NAMES`, `H-A-SCH-HIER-ECO`, `H-A-SCH-HARN-OPEN`, `H-A-SCH-HARN-FILE`,
`H-A-SCH-HARN-NETS` and `H-A-SCH-HARN-UNUSED`. Only the maintainer's author report of Part H of
`docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md` settles them: no `kicad-cli` path reads a schematic document, so there
is no oracle. Fenolite's own readback (`tests/_altium_read.py`, `nets_from_project`) proves that the written
sheets join the model's nets under the hierarchical scope and raises no label. The records were checked
against files that Altium saved in two public repositories (S-0187, S-0188), read in a scratch folder and
never committed. The build envelope stays `INFERRED`.

The maintainer's report of Part H (2026-10-03, AD 26.5) confirms `H-A-SCH-HIER-OPEN`, `H-A-SCH-HARN-OPEN`
and `H-A-SCH-HARN-UNUSED`. Steps H4 and H5 were not reported in detail. Step H3 gave "multiple names"
warnings, after which the top sheet was changed to a signal harness line between the two sheet entries.
Step H7 found one module sheet outside the hierarchy. The follow-up first registered `H-A-SCHBIN-MINI`, a
guess from sizes: the sheet outside was the only module sheet whose `FileHeader` stream lay in the mini
stream, and small sheets were padded. The maintainer's second report of step H7 (2026-10-03, AD 26.5)
refutes it: the padded sheets changed nothing, and in every project only the first module sheet listed
was a child. `H-A-SCH-HIER-ORDER` succeeds it and is confirmed by variant l: with the minimal project
file the schematic documents are listed first and together. The padding is removed, `H-A-SCH-HIER-PRJ` is
restated for the order written now. The third report (2026-10-03, AD 26.5) repeated step H7 on the
rebuilt board example: both module sheets are under the top sheet after "Validate PCB Project" alone,
and the change order lists no component, pin or net change. It confirms `H-A-SCH-HIER-PRJ` for that
project and `H-A-SCH-HIER-ECO` for parts on module sheets. Steps H3, H4 and H5 on the rebuilt hierarchy
sample were not reported, so `H-A-SCH-HIER-COMPILE`, `H-A-SCH-HIER-NAMES`, `H-A-SCH-HARN-FILE` and
`H-A-SCH-HARN-NETS` stay pending.

Change c0085 (the complete Altium PCB document) adds `H-A-PCBX-READBACK`, `H-A-PCBX-STACK`,
`H-A-PCBX-VIASPAN`, `H-A-PCBX-TEXT`, `H-A-PCBX-KEEPOUT`, `H-A-PCBX-HOLE`, `H-A-PCBX-REPOUR` and
`H-A-PCBX-KICAD`. The ninth row of its design, about component bodies, is not registered: bodies were cut
from the change and are not written.

Change c0038 (Altium PCB copper) adds `H-A-PCB-CU-KICAD`, `H-A-PCB-CU-ROUNDTRIP`, `H-A-PCB-CU-TRACK`,
`H-A-PCB-CU-VIA`, `H-A-PCB-CU-STACK`, `H-A-PCB-CU-PLANE`, `H-A-PCB-CU-REPOUR`, `H-A-PCB-CU-CLASS`,
`H-A-PCB-CU-RULES` and `H-A-PCB-CU-VIEWER`. `H-A-PCB-CU-KICAD` is settled by the `kicad-cli pcb import` of
`tests/kicad/altium/test_pcbdoc_copper_oracle.py` and `H-A-PCB-CU-ROUNDTRIP` by the round trip of
`tests/kicad/altium/test_copper_from_oracle.py` (10.0.x only). The other eight rows are settled only by
the maintainer's report of Part C of `docs/evidence/altium-pcb.md`: the report of 2026-10-03 (AD 26.5)
confirms the repour, class and rules rows, and leaves observations on the track, via, stack and plane
rows; the Viewer row is not reported. Net classes, rules and a plane's net have
no oracle: KiCad's import writes no project file and makes no zone from a plane. The oracles check what
KiCad's importer reads, so the build envelope stays `INFERRED`.

Change c0048 (Altium change order) adds `H-A-ECO-NETCLASS`, `H-A-ECO-PRJ-KEYS`, `H-A-ECO-COMPCLASS`,
`H-A-ECO-ROOMS`, `H-A-ECO-SUPPLY` and `H-A-ECO-SHEETCLASS`, after the maintainer's report of 2026-10-03 (AD 26.5):
"Update PCB Document" on the board example proposed to remove the net class, and to add component classes,
rooms and "Supply Nets" rules. They are settled only by the maintainer's reports of Part E of
`docs/evidence/altium-pcb.md`. The report of 2026-10-04 confirms the first five on the board example with
module sheets, whose change order lists only the two "Supply Nets" rules. The flat routed sample of that
session kept its net class but was offered a component class named after its sheet and a room;
`H-A-ECO-SHEETCLASS`, registered that day, states the class and the key written since. The repeat of
the same day on the rebuilt samples confirms it: both change orders, the flat one included, list only the
two "Supply Nets" rules. The ticks of the tab "Class Generation" (step E2) were reported in neither
session, so `H-A-ECO-PRJ-KEYS` stays confirmed by its effect only.
`H-A-ECO-SUPPLY` states a difference that Fenolite accepts: no permitted source holds the rule's record, so
it is not written. `kicad-cli` reads no Altium schematic and imports no
class, so no oracle covers these rows.

Change c0013 (read-only check v0, inspect and doctor) adds `H-K-CHECK-COPYSET`, `H-K-CHECK-CANARY`,
`H-K-CLI-HELP` and `H-K-CHECK-ERC`. The first three gate the design and are settled by the `check-*` probes
on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 before the oracle relies on them, each with a fallback decided in the design: a wider
copy set, a two-run canary or another placement, and `"unknown"` matrix rows. `H-K-CHECK-ERC` only bounds
the claim of the `erc.lite` heuristics and stays pending until `sch erc` replaces them. The copy set and
the help grammar hold on both majors. The canary fires and is silenced as claimed, but its tracks change
other violations of large demo boards on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, so `H-K-CHECK-CANARY` is refuted and both
majors take the two-run fallback (`H-K-CHECK-CANARY-2`). `oracle.EVIDENCE` and `helpmatrix.EVIDENCE` are
`KICAD-VERIFIED`.

Change c0019 (layout preservation) adds `H-K-LENS-KEEP`, settled by the layout oracle in
`tests/kicad/lens/` (a footprint moved by token edit, re-saved by `pcb upgrade --force` on 10.0.6, survives
a rebuild with its tracks intact, judged by `pcb export pos` and an unchanged DRC report), and
`H-K-LENS-FILL`, the premise of the fill digests, which c0015 settles by refilling a rebuilt board.
`lens.preserve.EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED`: it covers the edited blink, not every board.

Change c0020 (DRC findings, assignment compare, RT2 and format measurements) adds `H-K-DRC-TYPES`,
`H-K-PRO-SEV` and `H-K-DRC-UUID`, settled by probes on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 before DRC violations become
located issues; `H-K-NET-IPC`, settled before the assignment comparison relies on the IPC-D-356 export;
`H-K-RT2-STABLE`, which the corpus run refuted (KiCad does not repeat its DRC report on large boards), and
its successor `H-K-RT2-STABLE-2`, which bounds the RT2 verdict; `H-K-DRC-MEASURED`, registered only for the
v0.3 analysis change; and `H-K-TOK-CENSUS` and `H-G-EDGE-EXACT`, settled by measurements that never gate.
Each gating row has a fallback decided in the design: positions instead of `REF-PIN`, the report's own
severity, or a `-2` successor for the major that differs.

Change c0051 (DRC repeatability and the two-run canary test) adds `H-K-DRC-LIMIT`, `H-K-DRC-REPEAT` and
`H-K-CHECK-CANARY-3`. A stress run on the demo boards found the cause of the occasional failure of
`test_two_run_demo_boards`: `kicad-cli` stops reporting `clearance` violations near 499 per run, and on a
board with more the canary's own violation can be left out, so `H-K-CHECK-CANARY-2` is refuted by its own
criterion. Its successor adds the limit: a canary missing from a saturated report is `inconclusive`
(`clearance-limit`), never `absent`. `H-K-DRC-LIMIT` is settled on both majors by an authored bench before
the oracle relies on `CLEARANCE_REPORT_LIMIT`. `H-K-DRC-REPEAT` states what two DRC runs share: everything
but the report order and the entries of three named types, which bounds the determinism promise of
`fenolite check`. `oracle.EVIDENCE` keeps its level and cites the successor.

Change c0024 (manufacturing exports) adds `H-K-EXPORT-FILES`, `H-K-EXPORT-REPEAT` and `H-K-EXPORT-RENDER`, settled by the `export-*` probes on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 before `exports.plan` relies on
them. Fenolite claims the set of files an export writes and their hashes, never the fabrication data: a
file whose name the plan does not expect is still listed, a kind that fails writes nothing, and a view
that cannot be rendered is a warning. The three hold on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, so `exports.EVIDENCE` and the
plot evidence are `KICAD-VERIFIED`.

Change c0021 (KiCad libraries cache) settles `H-K-LIB-RELPATH`, `H-K-LIB-FALLBACK`, `H-K-LIB-NESTED` and
`H-K-LIB-CONFIGHOME` with `kicad-cli` probes whose control is an altered placement: its one
`lib_footprint_mismatch` fires only when the library was found, so an empty report is never read as
success. The probes refuted `H-K-LIB-RELPATH`: KiCad resolves a relative uri against its working
directory, not against the folder of the table. Its successor `H-K-LIB-RELPATH-2` states that rule, and
the resolver now joins a relative uri to the project folder. The change adds `H-K-LIB-COMMON`, which gates the opt-in reading of `kicad_common.json` (the rule is
removed if the probe fails on 10.0.6), and `H-K-LIB-SCAN`, which checks that a directory scan of an
official library tree gives the nicknames of its template table and stays `INFERRED`: the official
libraries are one origin.

Change c0028 (board-frame queries and manual copper) adds `H-G-FRAME-UUID`, `H-G-FRAME-SHAPE`,
`H-G-FRAME-CRTYD` and `H-G-FRAME-ROUTE`. The first gates the design: script copper is told from copper drawn
in KiCad by a version-8 uuid with a Fenolite marker, so the uuid probes run on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 before any
resolution code, with a `uuid5` fallback decided in the design. The shape and courtyard rows are settled by
canaries with negative controls at ±20 µm on a bench of authored footprints, and cover that bench only:
`frame.EVIDENCE` and `copper.EVIDENCE` stay `INFERRED`. The four hold on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, except that
circle courtyards need 40 µm of overlap: `H-G-FRAME-CRTYD` is refuted for them and succeeded by
`H-G-FRAME-CRTYD-2`, the extent rule unchanged. Board-frame pad positions register no row: they are
the formulas of `H-G-ROT-DIR`, `H-G-BOTTOM-PLACE` and `H-G-PAD-ANGLE-ABS`, re-checked at 180° and 270°.

Change c0030 (footprint fields) adds `H-K-FIELD-FRAME`, `H-K-FIELD-JUSTIFY`, `H-K-FIELD-DRC`,
`H-K-FIELD-RESAVE`, `H-K-FIELD-V8` and `H-K-FIELD-OUTSIDE`. The first three fix the frame the model uses for
a footprint field (a footprint-local anchor, a board angle in the file, justification in the reading frame
of the text) and are settled only by `pcb drc` canaries on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6: probes on boards written as
text run before the model exists, with a stop rule. `H-K-FIELD-OUTSIDE` covers the bench references only, so
`fields.EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED`. `H-K-FIELD-RESAVE` refines `H-K-UUID-KEEP-2`: a 10.0.6 re-save turns
only hidden `fp_text user` items into properties. `H-K-FIELD-V8` stays `INFERRED`: no source read states how
8.0 writes a hidden property, and the reader accepts both spellings.
The probes and the oracle of 2026-10-04 hold on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, so the frame, justification, DRC and
outside rows are `KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x)` and the re-save row `KICAD-VERIFIED (10.0.x)`; one control
of the outside row was amended (sideways anchors 1 mm inside the box), and no row was refuted.

Change c0031 (zone settings) adds `H-K-ZONE-DEFAULTS`, `H-K-ZONE-CONNECT`, `H-K-ZONE-GEOM`, `H-K-ZONE-FORM`,
`H-K-ZONE-FAT9` and `H-K-ZONE-LOAD9`. The defaults and the target-9 plot gate the design: their probes run on
10.0.6 and 9.0.9 before the model code, with a stop rule. The meaning of each setting is settled by refills on
10.0.6, judged with the exact kernel; 9.0 has no `--refill-zones`, so on 9.0.9 only loading is observed. The
forms per major are settled by the corpus census and RT1. All six hold on 2026-10-04 (10.0.6 locally, 9.0.9 in the pinned image); the
`kicad-9` and `kicad-10` jobs confirm them when the change is pushed.

Change c0029 (copper check) adds `H-K-VIA-RENET`, `H-K-COPPER-SHAPES`, `H-K-COPPER-RESOLVE` and
`H-K-COPPER-ZONES`. The first records a report: a via of one net touching a track of another was said to
load on the track's net. The probes confirm it on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6 for a track whose net owns a pad, and show that
copper without pads is merged into one net whose name differs between runs. Nothing depends on the row:
Fenolite's own check catches the short before any tool reads the board. The three `H-K-COPPER-*` rows are
settled by parity canaries on authored benches, per pair kind and per clearance source, and cover those
benches only, so `checks.copper.EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED`.

Change c0015 (zone fill) adds `H-K-FILL-SAVE`, `H-K-FILL-LIFT`, `H-K-FILL-REPEAT`,
`H-K-FILL-LOAD9` and `H-K-CLI-DOCKER`. The first four are settled by version-pinned
refill, lift and load probes; the Docker row records whether the pinned image matches the local tool.
Change c0022 (placement) adds `H-K-PLACE-MOVE`, `H-K-PLACE-TOUCH` and `H-G-PLACE-OUTLINE`. The first two are
probes that run before the code relies on them: a footprint moved by `move_footprint` must read back at the
requested placement with its pad nets, and KiCad decides whether courtyards that only touch overlap. The
third is a measurement over the demo boards and never gates. A placement is never a verdict, so
`placement.EVIDENCE` stays `INFERRED` until both `H-K-PLACE-*` rows are verified on both majors; they are
since 2026-10-04, so it is `KICAD-VERIFIED`. `H-G-PLACE-OUTLINE` stays `INFERRED`: 3 of the 21 demo boards
have an outline in KiCad and no ring here.

Change c0016 (routing plugins) adds `H-K-KRT-CLI`, `H-K-KRT-ROUTE`, `H-K-KRT-KEEP` and `H-K-KRT-REPEAT`. The first three check the pinned router's command, route quality and preservation of non-copper board data; the last records repeatability without making it a gate.

Change c0023 (Specctra and Freerouting) adds `H-G-DSN-ACCEPT`, `H-G-DSN-UNITS`, `H-G-DSN-PROTECT`,
`H-G-DSN-ROUTE`, `H-G-DSN-REPEAT` and `H-G-DSN-OFFLINE`. The first three are settled by Freerouting 2.4.1
itself on files Fenolite writes, the fourth by KiCad's DRC on the routed blink, and the last two record an
outcome either way. The writer and the reader were built before the probes (the maintainer's decision of
2026-10-04 to work in parallel with c0016), so every fact of `docs/formats/specctra/` was written as `INFERRED`.
`H-G-DSN-INCOMPLETE` was added the same day by the acceptance loop of c0025: a session says nothing about
a connection the router did not make. The six rows were settled on 2026-10-04 by local runs (the offline one in the pinned image); the fact pages keep
`INFERRED` on every row until each fact is matched to what its probe exercises, which is not done yet.

Change c0047 (board analyses) adds `H-G-AN-FIT`, `H-G-AN-VIA`, `H-G-AN-GAP`, `H-G-AN-PATH` and
`H-G-AN-EDGE`, which stay `INFERRED`: the capacity fit has no command-line oracle, and the distance
measures are checked by hand-computed authored cases and a grid search. `H-K-AN-CREEP` records whether
KiCad's `creepage` rule brackets Fenolite's value on two benches. It was refuted: KiCad agrees around a slot
on one face and reports nothing across the board edge, which `H-K-AN-CREEP-2` states. Both rows are
supporting data and raise no label.
Change c0025 (release v0.1) adds `H-K-REL-LOOP40` and `H-G-REL-WINDOWS`. The first says that the
acceptance loop closes on the second example board with the router alone; the second that the hermetic
suites are portable to Windows. Each has a recorded fallback, so neither blocks the release record: it
states what was proved. The first Windows run failed 35 tests; Windows stays in v0.1 by
the maintainer's decision, so they are fixed.

Change c0040 (Altium schematic reader) adds `H-A-RD-SCH-FRAME`, `H-A-RD-SCH-IDENT`, `H-A-RD-SCH-HEADER`,
`H-A-RD-SCH-CASE`, `H-A-RD-SCH-TEXT`, `H-A-RD-SCH-OWNER`, `H-A-RD-SCH-ADDOWNER`, `H-A-RD-SCH-LIBOWNER`,
`H-A-RD-SCH-FRAC`, `H-A-RD-SCH-PARTS`, `H-A-RD-SCH-PIN`, `H-A-RD-SCH-PINSIDE`, `H-A-RD-SCH-STORAGE`,
`H-A-RD-SCH-ASCII` and `H-A-RD-SCH-KICAD`, and `H-A-RD-SCH-TEXT-2` after the census refuted `H-A-RD-SCH-TEXT`. The structural rows are settled by the corpus test and the census of
public files from three repositories of schematics and three of libraries; `H-A-RD-SCH-PIN`, `H-A-RD-SCH-PARTS`
and `H-A-RD-SCH-KICAD` also by the library oracle, for the fields it compares only. `H-A-RD-SCH-ASCII` stays
`INFERRED`: no public Altium-saved ASCII schematic is known.

Change c0046 (Altium sheet-template import) adds the eight `H-A-RD-SHT-*` rows: `H-A-RD-SHT-SAME`, `-OWNER`,
`-AREA`, `-BORDER`, `-WIDTH`, `-TEXT`, `-STRINGS` and `-IMAGE`. All stay `INFERRED`. The corpus result of
three public templates of two repositories is supporting data only: three repositories are needed for
`CORPUS-VERIFIED`, and no `kicad-cli` path reads an Altium schematic. The oracle of the change checks the
written `.kicad_wks` (`H-K-WKS-CORNER`), not these rows. The `kicad-9` and `kicad-10` jobs ran that oracle and
the corpus tests of the change on Linux (run https://github.com/lgili/Fenolite/actions/runs/37291111078).

Change c0041 (Altium PCB reader) adds the twelve `H-A-RD-PCB-*` rows: `H-A-RD-PCB-FRAME`, `-IDENTITY`,
`-LENGTHS`, `-REGION`, `-TEXT`, `-RULE`, `-POLYNAME` and `-CODEC` are settled by the corpus census of
eleven public Altium-saved files (`tests/corpus/test_altium_pcb_census.py`), and `H-A-RD-PCB-KICAD-DOC`,
`-KICAD-LIB`, `-PAD` and `-STACK` by `kicad-cli pcb import` and `fp upgrade` (`tests/kicad/altium/`). No row
needs an author report: the reader writes nothing. The document oracle also settles `H-A-UNIT`.

Change c0064 (BOM and placement tables) adds `H-K-BOM-CSV`, `H-K-BOM-MODEL` and `H-K-POS-ROWS`. The two BOM
rows needed a schematic that `kicad-cli` can export from; they were settled on 2026-10-06, on the
schematics that `build` writes since c0061. `H-K-POS-ROWS` extends
`H-K-PCB-POS` from positions to whole rows (value, package name, side) and to the rendered decimals.
Change c0066 (CLI inspection commands) adds `H-K-FMT-IDEMPOTENT`: the canonical print that `fenolite fmt` writes
is a fixed point over the corpus. It claims nothing about KiCad's own printer, which the other `H-K-FMT-*`
rows measure.
Change c0060 (KiCad schematic reader) adds `H-K-SCH-READ`, `H-K-SCH-RT1`, `H-K-SCH-COMPONENTS` and
`H-K-SCH-TOKENS`. The first two are settled by the rebuild of the authored fixtures and by RT0 and RT1 over
the schematic rows of the corpus; the third by the `sch-components-*` probes, which compare the components
Fenolite reads with those `kicad-cli sch export netlist` lists on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, and was refuted on the corpus:
its successor `H-K-SCH-COMPONENTS-2` resolves symbols by instance path; the fourth by the fuzz
results of the inventory rows of kinds `kicad_sch` and `kicad_sym`.
Change c0061 (KiCad schematic writer) adds `H-K-SCH-MINIMAL`, `H-K-SCH-PINFRAME`, `H-K-SCH-UNCONNECTED`,
`H-K-SCH-SLASH`, `H-K-SCH-PARITY`, `H-K-SCH-POWER`, `H-K-SCH-LIBTABLE`, `H-K-SCH-RESAVE` and `H-K-SCH-UPDATE`:
what a generated schematic must hold for KiCad's ERC and parity test to report nothing. The first eight are
settled by the probes of `tests/kicad/schematic/test_naming_probes.py` and by the generated-project oracle on
9.0.9 and 10.0.6, all confirmed on 2026-10-05; the last one has no headless tool and stays `INFERRED`.
Change c0063 (netlist compare) adds `H-K-NETLIST-SHAPE` and `H-K-NETLIST-OWN`: the parts of KiCad's
netlist export that `netlist.read_netlist` reads, and the equality of Fenolite's own netlist of a
generated sheet with that export. They are settled by `tests/kicad/schematic/test_netlist_facts.py` and
`test_own_netlist.py` on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6; both were confirmed on 2026-10-05 in the pinned image and on
the local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6.
Change c0062 (ERC oracle) adds `H-K-ERC-JSON`, `H-K-ERC-POS`, `H-K-ERC-TYPES`, `H-K-ERC-COPYSET`,
`H-K-ERC-REPEAT`, `H-K-ERC-RT2` and `H-K-PARITY-RUN`: what `kicad-cli sch erc` writes and reads, and what
`pcb drc --schematic-parity` adds to a DRC report. They are settled by the probes of
`tests/kicad/check/test_erc_facts.py`, `test_erc_oracle.py` and `test_parity.py` on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, and by
the schematic RT2 run over the corpus (`tests/kicad/schematic/test_corpus_rt2.py`). That run refuted
`H-K-ERC-REPEAT` and `H-K-ERC-RT2` on 2026-10-06: KiCad names another item of one violation from run to
run, so their successors `H-K-ERC-REPEAT-2` and `H-K-ERC-RT2-2` compare the sheet, type, severity and
exclusion of every violation and leave the items out.
Change c0068 (follow-ups of v0.1) adds `H-G-FRAME-OFFSET`, `H-K-COPPER-ZONECLR`, `H-G-FRAME-ARC`,
`H-K-COPPER-VIAKINDS`, `H-K-PAD-ZONE-LIB` and `H-K-CI-MACOSAPP`. The first corrects a fact that
`H-G-FRAME-SHAPE` covered without a test of it: the offset of a pad's drill moves the pad's copper, not its
hole. All six start `INFERRED` with the measurements of the change's design as their first record.
On 2026-10-05 the first five were confirmed on the local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6, and on 2026-10-06 on 9.0.9,
in the pinned image and by the `kicad-9` job, so they stand at `KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x)`.
`H-K-CI-MACOSAPP` was confirmed on 2026-10-06 by the first run of the `macos-app` job, once `nightly.yml`
was on the default branch.
Change c0071 (full rule kinds) adds `H-K-DRU-KIND-2`, `H-K-DRU-COURTYARD` and `H-K-PRO-MIN-RULE-3`, settled by
the rules oracle in `tests/kicad/rules/` on benches whose canary is scoped to its own net, because an
unscoped clearance canary takes the one violation KiCad reports per item pair. `rulemap.KIND_SUPPORT` is
pinned to the `dru-kind-*` outcomes, so a kind is written only for the majors that enforce it.

Change c0073 (interfaces and quantities) adds `H-K-DIFFPAIR-NAMES`, settled by
`tests/kicad/rules/test_diffpair_names.py` with one `inDiffPair()` rule per pair of names; the build
warning `build.diff-pair-name` applies the same rule to the nets of `diff_pair` and `usb2` interfaces.

Change c0069 (complete layout lens) adds `H-K-LENS-RENAME`, settled by
`tests/kicad/lens/test_rename_oracle.py` on the lens acceptance fixture: a module renamed through
`moved()` keeps its footprints, with their uuids moved to the new path, and KiCad reads the result as
the same board.
Change c0045 (design equivalence) adds the twelve `H-G-EQ-*` rows: `H-G-EQ-L1` to `H-G-EQ-L4` say that
Fenolite's import of an Altium-written PCB document and `kicad-cli`'s import of it are equivalent at each
level of `fenolite equivalent`, and `H-G-EQ-SHIFT`, `-ROUND`, `-REF`, `-FPNAME`, `-VALUE`, `-ROT`, `-PADSHAPE`
and `-FREE` name what KiCad's importer is expected to change. They are settled by the triangle
(`tests/kicad/equivalence/`), whose results are in `docs/evidence/equivalence-triangle.md`. The first
measurement (2026-10-05, `kicad-cli` 10.0.6) refuted `H-G-EQ-ROUND` and `H-G-EQ-FREE`, whose successors
`H-G-EQ-ROUND-2` and `H-G-EQ-FREE-2` say what was measured, and found one fault of Fenolite's adapter, fixed in
the change: a component of a board takes the designator text the board shows. The four level rows stay
`INFERRED` while an `undecided` rule matches; no `H-A-*` row changes its level because of the triangle.

Change c0074 (drawing sheets and follow-ups) adds `H-K-PRO-WKS-SCH`, `H-K-OUTLINE-CHAIN`,
`H-K-OUTLINE-FPEDGE`, `H-K-EXPORT-OPTIONS` and `H-K-STITCH-AVOID`, settled by
`tests/kicad/followups/test_followup_probes.py`, and refutes `H-G-EDGE-EXACT`: KiCad closes an outline
across gaps below 10 µm. `H-K-EXPORT-OPTIONS` stays `INFERRED`: six preset keys change nothing on the
probe board.

Change c0044 (Altium verification) adds the six `H-A-VER-*` rows: `H-A-VER-RTA0`, `-WRITER`, `-RTA1` and
`-BYTES` for the container copy and the record round trip over the public corpus, `-RTA2` for the built
examples read back inside the written scope, and `-ERC` for `erc.lite` on an imported schematic. All start
`INFERRED`. The corpus run of 2026-10-05 confirms `-WRITER`, `-BYTES` and `-ERC`; `-RTA0` and `-RTA1` pass on every
row and stay pending, because the PCB libraries of the corpus come from two repositories. The first probe of the example builds
refuted `H-A-VER-RTA2`: the built model of an Altium build holds no placed footprint and no copper, so the
row's successor `H-A-VER-RTA2-2` bounds the claim to what the built model holds.
Change c0072 (schematic and board parity) adds `H-K-PARITY-TYPES` and `H-K-PARITY-OWN`: which types KiCad's
parity test gives for each board edit, and that the counts of `checks.parity.compare` equal KiCad's. They are
settled by `tests/kicad/check/test_parity_probes.py` and `test_parity_agreement.py` on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6; both
were confirmed on 2026-10-06 in the pinned image and on the local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6. `checks.parity.EVIDENCE`
stays `INFERRED` and names `H-K-PARITY-OWN`: the public demos are not every project.

Change c0070 (schematic hierarchy and readable layout) adds `H-K-SCH-HIER-FILE`, `H-K-SCH-HIER-PATH` and
`H-K-SCH-WIRE-END`: how KiCad finds the file of a child sheet, which path it gives a symbol of a child
sheet, and what a wire joins. They are settled by `tests/kicad/schematic/test_hierarchy_probes.py` and
`test_hierarchy_oracle.py` on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6; all three were confirmed on 2026-10-06 in the pinned image and on
the local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6. What "Update PCB from Schematic" writes for a part of a module was not measured.

Change c0089 (equivalence level 5) adds `H-G-EQ-L5`, `H-G-EQ-L5-TRIANGLE` and `H-G-EQ-L5-SPLIT`: level 5
of `fenolite equivalent` finds each of five planted routing edits, a routed KiCad board equals the Altium
document written from it and KiCad's import of that document, and re-segmented tracks are the same copper.
The triangle was measured on 2026-10-06 with `kicad-cli` 10.0.6 (`docs/evidence/equivalence-triangle.md`,
"Level 5, kicad-cli 10.0"). `H-G-EQ-L5-SPLIT` was refuted in its bound (2 nm per segment end does not cover
a diagonal segment) and is superseded by `H-G-EQ-L5-SPLIT-2`. No `H-A-*` row changes its level.

Change c0087 (Altium output job and sheet template) adds `H-A-OUTJOB-READBACK`, `-OPEN`, `-RUN` and `-OPTIONS`
and `H-A-SCHDOT-READBACK`, `-OPEN` and `-STRINGS`. The two readback rows are settled by unit tests on Fenolite's
own readers and stay `INFERRED`: a reader and a writer of one project agreeing is supporting data, not a
second opinion. The five others wait for Parts O and W of `docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`.

Change c0138 (the Gerber settings record of the written output job) adds `H-A-OUTJOB-GERBER-EMPTY`, `-RECORD`,
`-READBACK`, `-ACCEPT`, `-LAYERS`, `-PLANE` and `-DECIMALS`, and the successors `H-A-OUTJOB-RUN-2` and
`H-A-OUTJOB-OPTIONS-2` of the two rows of c0087 that were written for a job without settings. All nine are `INFERRED`.
The two old rows keep their statements, their levels and the observation of 2026-10-07; neither is marked refuted:
that needs the maintainer's stated outcome with his minor version. The record and read-back rows rest on tests
over two public jobs and on Fenolite's own reader, which is supporting data; the six others wait for Part O,
session 2, of `docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`.

Change c0086 (the complete Altium schematic) adds `H-A-SCHX-READBACK`, `H-A-SCHX-GRAPHICS`, `H-A-SCHX-TREE`,
`H-A-SCHX-DIR`, `H-A-SCHX-BUS`, `H-A-SCHX-TEXT` and `H-A-SCHX-ECO`. The first is the writer's own readback
row; the other six wait for the report of Part Y of `docs/evidence/altium-schematic.md`. Since that change
the default build draws symbols from their own graphics, and the schematics and schematic libraries of the
samples `blink`, `kicad_example`, `no_connect` and `routed` were regenerated. Every author report recorded
before 2026-10-06 on one of those samples (rows `H-A-SCH-NC-*`, `H-A-SCHLIB-*`, `H-A-PCB-*`, `H-A-ECO-*`)
covered its rectangle form, which `tests/data/altium/generic/` keeps byte for byte and
`--altium-symbols generic` still builds. No level of those rows changed, and none says anything about the
graphics form.

Change c0090 (Altium write of a model) adds `H-A-VER-RTA2-3`, `H-A-VER-RTA3`, `H-A-VER-RTA3-PRJ` and
`H-A-VER-RTA3-KICAD`. `H-A-VER-RTA2-3` replaces `H-A-VER-RTA2-2`, whose clause about board kinds that are
only counted no longer describes the stage: the model that an Altium build stores now holds the board
that was written. The RT-A3 rows say that Fenolite's write and Fenolite's import agree on the model of a
document that Altium saved, inside the written scope; none of them says that Altium opens a rewritten
document.

Change c0128 (vias with a drill equal to the diameter in a rewrite) adds `H-A-PCBX-VIA-FULL`: a fact about
what a saved document holds, read from one public document, on which the relaxed rule of a rewrite rests.
It says nothing about how Altium treats such a via.

Change c0132 (vias without a pad shape on inner layers) adds `H-A-IMP-VIA-PADLESS`: where a via record says
that the via has no pad on a layer. The layout is measured; the meaning fits the copper of the one public
document that holds such records and Altium's documentation of the tool, and the index of the table (by
layer id) is not settled by that document. The copper check on Altium input rests on it.

Change c0121 (extruded component bodies in the Altium PCB document) adds eight rows `H-A-PCBX-BODY-*`. `-FORM` and `-2D`
are facts about what saved documents hold, measured on the public corpus; their evidence is thin: 1272 extruded
records of five documents of three repositories, 1265 of them from one repository, and nothing that Fenolite writes
was opened in Altium. `-READBACK` is Fenolite reading what Fenolite wrote, and `-KICAD` says that KiCad's import is
undisturbed and shows no body. `-OPEN`, `-SHORT`, `-ID` and `-LIB` wait for step X8 of the author's second Altium
session. No row says that Altium accepts the two stand-in values (`MODELID`, `MODEL.CHECKSUM`): that is `-OPEN`,
which is pending, and it is why bodies are written only on request.

Change c0141 (DRC report limits marked per type in `check`) adds `H-K-DRC-LIMITS`: where KiCad's DRC report stops, per type. It is measured with an authored bench; no public statement of the limits was found. `check` rests on it when it marks a count as a lower bound (`summary.limits`, `check.report-limit`), and the canary's reason `clearance-limit` reads the same table. `H-K-DRC-LIMIT` (singular, c0051) is the canary's claim about `clearance` alone and keeps its level.
Change c0108 (routing of open nets) adds `H-K-CONN-PARITY`, `H-K-LOCK-FORM`, `H-G-DSN-PARTIAL`,
`H-K-KRT-PARTIAL` and `H-A-PCB-CU-LOCK`. The first says that the open connections Fenolite counts from
the board are KiCad's unconnected items per net: it holds on the bench and on the corpus under 10.0.6, and
its 9.0.9 half was measured when the change was proposed and waits for the committed tests in CI. The
second is where KiCad writes the lock of a track, an arc and a via. The two router rows say what a router
does with a net that already holds copper; neither gates anything, because `route` takes its verdict from
the board. Freerouting closed the five kinds of the bench and left one more case open, a pad to be joined
to the middle of one protected track, which the verdict reports as open. The last is the lock bit of a free primitive in an Altium document: it stays `INFERRED` until
the author's report of step X12.
Change c0116 (document exports) adds `H-K-EXPORT-DOCS`, `H-K-EXPORT-DOCS-REPEAT`, `H-K-EXPORT-MODELS`,
`H-K-EXPORT-SHEETS`, `H-K-EXPORT-PDF-PAGE` and `H-G-MODELS-FETCH`. The five `H-K-*` rows are settled by the
`export-files-*`, `export-repeat-*`, `export-models-*`, `export-sheets-missing` and `export-pdf-page` probes of the
six document kinds; they are recorded for `kicad-cli` 10.0.6 and not yet for 9.0.9, so every row is `INFERRED`,
and with them `exports.DOCUMENTS_EVIDENCE` and `backends.kicad.models.EVIDENCE`. `H-G-MODELS-FETCH` waited for the
first real fetch of official 3D models, which needed the maintainer's consent: given on 2026-10-08, the fetch
of the models of `examples/blink_official` at both tags settled it. On 2026-10-08 the five `H-K-*` rows are
`KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x)` (the image run and CI run 37772583226), and the two constants follow.
Change c0109 (routing scale control) adds `H-G-DSN-NOOPT`, `H-G-DSN-NETLESS`, `H-G-DSN-NETLESS-2` and
`H-K-KRT-GROUP`. The first is why the Freerouting plugin passes the setting that stops the optimizer: the
session is then written when the autorouter ends, so a run inside the budget leaves copper. The second was
the claim that a net left out of the design file is still kept clear of by its own class clearance; the
probe refuted that half (the router keeps the default rule from copper without a net), so `-2` states what
was measured and `write_dsn` keeps a net of a wider class declared. `H-K-KRT-GROUP` is the grouped run of
KiCadRoutingTools; the routing jobs of CI ran `test_group` on both majors (run 37772583226), so it is `KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x)`.
Change c0111 (copper in a part's frame) adds `H-G-FRAME-ANCHOR` and `H-K-VIA-IN-PAD`: where a point given in a
footprint's library frame lies for KiCad, shown by the net KiCad gives a marker via inside a pad (`H-K-VIA-RENET`),
and KiCad's verdict on through vias of a pad's net inside an SMD pad. Both were measured on 10.0.6 on 2026-10-07 and
stay `INFERRED` until the same probes ran on 9.0.9; the rows they build on keep their levels.

Change c0104 (differential pair constraints) adds `H-K-DRU-PAIR`, `H-K-DRU-PAIRSEL`, `H-K-DIFFPAIR-NAMES-2`,
`H-K-PRO-PAIR` and `H-K-COPPER-PAIR`. Their first record is the measurement of the change's design
(2026-10-05, scratch benches on 9.0.9 and 10.0.6, the same outcomes on both). On 2026-10-08 the committed
benches confirmed every row on the local `kicad-cli` 10.0.6; the 9.0.9 half of the probes is not recorded
yet, so the five rows stand at `KICAD-VERIFIED (10.0.x)`, and `rulemap.KIND_SUPPORT` and
`SELECTOR_SUPPORT["diff_pair"]` hold 10 only, as their probe files say. `H-K-DIFFPAIR-NAMES` keeps its level
for its eight cases; `-2` extends its rule to names with a tail of digits and underscores.
Change c0113 (placement rules) adds `H-K-PLACE-KEEPOUT`: how KiCad's DRC judges a footprint in a rule
area that forbids footprints. `place.keepout` of `placement.legality` follows its 18 cases, and
`KEEPOUT_EVIDENCE` follows the row. It stays `INFERRED` until the probes are recorded on both majors.

Change c0145 (the singular and plural layer heads of a zone) adds `H-K-ZONE-LAYER-HEAD`: which head KiCad uses for the
layers of a zone or rule area, what 10.0.6 loads and what it saves. The writer rests on it when it writes a changed layer
set by the number of layers. It was measured on 10.0.6 only; which program writes a wildcard under `layer` is not known.

The second image run of 2026-10-08 on `kicad-cli` 9.0.9 (the pinned image, local; CI has still to confirm it)
recorded the 173 probes that c0102, c0103, c0104, c0111, c0113, c0114 and c0117 registered for major 9. It moved 29
rows of these changes to `KICAD-VERIFIED (9.0.x, 10.0.x)`, each with the run in its result, and the sentences above
that say a row of c0104 or c0111 waits for 9.0.9 describe the state before it. Three rows did not move:
`H-K-OUTLINE-INVALID` (9.0.9 reports no `invalid_outline` for the self-crossing ring of the bench; its statement and the test now say so),
`H-K-PRO-SEV-KEYS` (its level is that of the 10.0.6 key set, by c0114's task) and `H-K-PLACE-KEEPOUT` (its criterion
is met, and its level moves together with `KEEPOUT_EVIDENCE`, which a test ties to it). `rulemap.KIND_SUPPORT` of the
five pair and length kinds and `SELECTOR_SUPPORT` of `area` and `diff_pair` hold both majors since that run.
Change c0118 (assembly and test features) adds `H-K-PAD-FABPROP`, `H-K-PAD-FABPROP-LIB`,
`H-K-FIDUCIAL-FORM`, `H-K-FIDUCIAL-KEEPOUT` and `H-K-TESTPOINT-D356`. The five were measured with the local
`kicad-cli` 10.0.6 on 2026-10-08 and every probe has the outcome the design expected; they stay `INFERRED`
until the same probes are recorded inside the pinned 9.0.9 image, and `exports.testpoints.EVIDENCE` stays
`INFERRED` with them. The fiducial and its keep-out were measured on a bench written through the model
API: the script calls that will generate them wait for changes c0102 and c0103.
Change c0153 (private state per `kicad-cli` run) adds `H-K-CLI-STATE`: where the tool keeps the instance
lock file that parallel runs on one machine raced on. The cause is read from the message the tool printed
in CI and from the failures being transient; the package runner and the tests' helpers give every run its
own temporary, runtime, cache and state folders whatever the row's outcome, and the row's oracle test, run
by the `kicad-9` and `kicad-10` jobs, measures the folder.

Change c0098 (electrical readiness) adds `H-G-READY-RULES`: the three rules that `fenolite ready` adds to the
findings it gathers (unconnected pins, power nets without a declared width or a zone, parts without a value).
They are definitions of Fenolite, judged on the model, so the row stays `INFERRED`; the open connections and
the ERC and DRC findings of the same reply keep the levels of `H-K-CONN-PARITY` and of their stages.
